2026-11-01T12:19:46.0086937Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 with P and rerun the split MegaMoE diagnostics with the fixed trace reader, on the round-18 image without a build

Round 18 (41296967433) served P at 10005.35 but its in-job MegaMoE
diagnostics returned no data: parse_trace found no single magic header
in the first 1024 words of the trace buffer on prefill-2, and the other
workers were cut with it.

Cause (by Codex, 794e669, from source and a GPU-free C++ check): host and
kernel agree that the normal buffer ends at 1,445,431,328 bytes, and the
kernel writes the private trace at the next 4096-byte boundary. With a
page-aligned allocation that header sits 8192 bytes, word 1024, past the
old read position, while the parser searched only words 0-1023. The
other candidates (layout mismatch, the normal variant being called,
missing synchronization, magic type) are refuted in source. Round 18
kept no raw buffer, so its exact placement is not proven.

Fix (runtime scripts only):
- read the trace at align_up(buffer address plus size, 4096), as the
  kernel does;
- keep the first 4096 trace bytes as hex, the buffer and trace
  addresses and sizes, offsets, tensor arguments, template and kernel
  name in every fragment; on a failure also the JIT source and the
  matching CUBIN symbols;
- record the full-time and byte/finite comparisons of all three
  routings even when parsing fails, and write rank exceptions into the
  fragment instead of letting torchrun take down the other ranks.
The 45 s bound, the grace periods, the prepack window and the
non-interference with P are unchanged.

No kernel, host, header, Dockerfile or build-script change, so this
reuses the round-18 image, which carries the diagnostic API. This
commit pins it as one set: images.sglang path, sha256
450ba3cc0194beeb02fe7359ab47c0acce1257cf4798afb148301088a0b53f68, size
12299833344 and provenance, container_build_run_id 41296967433,
container_sha256, container_size_bytes, container_source_commit 393c776,
PINNED_IMAGE in scripts/check-firmus-pp4-cpu.py, and the image prefixes
in the pair, prepack and diagnostic tests. P is in this image as in
5a3df8c4 and served from it in round 18. prefill.megamoe_diag stays
true; the FA3 gates are unchanged.

Validation: the full pytest suite exits 0 on this tree with no failures;
Codex reported 1440 passed and 78 skipped before the pin.
